Abstract: Quantum benchmarks provide compact measures of performance that are important for evaluating and comparing quantum systems. Circuit-level benchmarks are particularly valuable because they capture the accumulated effects of noise across interacting operations, but existing approaches may require structured gate sets and costly compilation, classical simulation of reference outputs, or subsystem decompositions that do not capture full-register behavior. We introduce Error Per Circuit Layer (EPCL), an overlap-based circuit-level benchmark that estimates an effective layer polarization by applying identical random circuits to two disjoint quantum registers and measuring the overlap between their output states as a function of circuit depth. EPCL avoids classical simulation of ideal output distributions and recovery to a known reference state, and is compatible with arbitrary gate sets, including non-Clifford gates.
  We derive the expected overlap decay under an ensemble-averaged depolarizing model and identify the assumptions under which the fitted decay parameter represents an effective layer polarization. Numerical simulations show that EPCL recovers the predicted polarization under weak local stochastic noise and remains well described by a single-exponential decay at stronger stochastic noise levels. The simulations further show that coherent errors associated with fixed entangling layers may require Pauli twirling or randomized compiling to produce the expected decay, while inter-register correlations contribute an additional covariance term to the measured overlap. Finally, experiments on IBM quantum hardware demonstrate clear EPCL decay in 8- and 16-qubit implementations. These results support EPCL as a method for measuring aggregate register performance without requiring classical simulation of ideal circuit outputs or restriction to structured gate sets.

</details>


### [98] [Efficient Conversion of Optical to Mechanical States Close to the Single-Quantum Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04150)
*Alexander Rolf Korsch,Liu Chen,Pedro V. Pinho,Boris Müllendorff,Jan N. Kirchhof,Yong Yu,Thiago P. Mayer Alegre,Simon Gröblacher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在毫开尔文温度下运行的腔光机械晶体中，演示了接近单量子水平的低噪声光子-声子态转换，实现了创纪录的η=0.76转换效率、7.3μs的机械存储寿命和超过4.5MHz的可调转换带宽，并通过Hanbury Brown-Twiss测量验证了转换后声子态的相干性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的光机械诱导透明（OMIT）转换协议受限于室温运行带来的热机械噪声，只能处理大相干态光子数的经典输入信号。为了实现对单量子水平机械态的确定性制备和混合量子信息处理，亟需在低温条件下演示低噪声的光子-声子量子态转换。

Method: 在毫开尔文温度下运行的光机械晶体中，利用弱相干光输入脉冲（少光子水平）实现光子到GHz机械模式的相干转换；通过Hanbury Brown-Twiss测量表征转化后声子态的相干性质并量化附加热噪声。

Result: 实验实现了η=0.76的光子-声子转换效率（创纪录水平），机械存储寿命T1=7.3μs，可调转换带宽超过4.5MHz；HBT测量表明转换后的声子态具有相干性，附加热噪声n_th=9.0，证明转换过程具备低噪声特性。

Conclusion: 这项工作确立了光机械晶体作为GHz机械模式高效光学接口的地位，为迈向确定性单量子水平机械态制备和混合量子信息处理铺平了道路。

Abstract: Coherent interfaces between optical photons and mechanical excitations provide a promising route towards phonon-state engineering and hybrid quantum information processing. Cavity optomechanical systems enable such interfaces via optomechanically induced transparency (OMIT), allowing coherent mapping between traveling optical fields and localized mechanical modes. However, previous implementations of OMIT conversion protocols were limited to classical input signals with large coherent state photon numbers due to room temperature operation and corresponding thermal mechanical noise. Here, we demonstrate efficient low-noise photon-phonon state transfer close to the single-quantum regime in an optomechanical crystal operated at Millikelvin temperatures. Using weak coherent optical input pulses at the few-photon level, we achieve a record-level photon-phonon conversion efficiency of $η=0.76$, a mechanical storage lifetime of $T_\mathrm{1}=7.3~μs$, and a tunable conversion bandwidth exceeding 4.5 MHz. Hanbury Brown-Twiss measurements of the retrieved signal demonstrate the coherent nature of the converted phononic state, evidencing low added thermal noise in the conversion process ($n_\mathrm{th}=9.0$). These results establish optomechanical crystals as efficient optical interfaces to GHz mechanical modes and provide a pathway toward deterministic single-quantum-level mechanical state preparation.

</details>


### [99] [Spurious quantum correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04157)
*Shashaank Khanna,Matthew F. Pusey,Roger Colbeck*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了"虚假量子关联"的概念——即某些因果结构中看起来是量子非经典关联，但可以在另一种无需微调的因果结构中获得经典解释。文章发现，贝尔因果结构中的非经典量子关联在所有替代因果结构中都需要微调，但其他因果结构中存在非经典量子关联在贝尔因果结构中无对应，其可替代性因结构而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔的工作展示了量子关联无法在贝尔因果结构中经典解释，但可以通过允许超光速因果影响的替代因果结构来解释，不过这些替代解释需要微调（有因果无关联）。本文希望探讨反方向的问题：是否存在量子关联看起来非经典，但在另一因果结构中能自然地经典解释，从而削弱"非经典量子关联"作为量子性判据的意义。

Method: 作者分析不同因果结构之间的兼容性关系，考察在某一因果结构中产生非经典量子关联的那些关联，是否可以在另一因果结构中无需微调地获得经典解释。通过对比贝尔因果结构与其他替代因果结构，将关联的"虚假性"分类讨论。

Result: 研究表明，存在所谓的"虚假量子关联"：在某些因果结构中看似量子非经典、但在另一种因果结构中具有无需微调的经典解释的关联。然而，类似贝尔因果结构中的非经典量子关联，也发现其他因果结构中的非经典量子关联在任何替代因果结构中都不存在无微调的经典解释。

Conclusion: 因果结构中的非经典量子关联并不总是意味着"不可替代的量子特征"，部分关联可能是虚假的，可在替代因果结构中获得自然的经典解释。但存在一类"鲁棒"的非经典量子关联，在任何替代因果结构中都无法无微调地经典解释，这部分关联才是真正体现量子非经典性的坚实证据。

Abstract: In his seminal paper, Bell [Physics Physique Fizika 1, 195 (1964)] considers the correlations that result from space-like separated measurements on a pair of entangled particles. He uses relativity theory to motivate the Bell causal structure, then shows the existence of quantum correlations that cannot be explained classically within this causal structure. Classical explanations of such quantum correlations are possible in alternative causal structures, for instance, those that allow superluminal causal influences, but, as shown in [New Journal of Physics 17 033002 (2015)], all such alternative explanations require fine tuning (causation without correlation). Here we discuss the existence of spurious quantum correlations --- correlations that look quantum in one causal structure, but have a natural classical explanation in another. More precisely, there are causal structures that admit non-classical quantum correlations, but for which the same correlations have a classical explanation in another causal structure without fine tuning. The realisation in the other causal structure can also be achieved without breaking any natural constraints on the causal structure that follow from relativity theory. However, similarly to non-classical quantum correlations in the Bell causal structure, we find other causal structures with non-classical quantum correlations that do not have a classical causal explanation in any alternative causal structure without fine tuning.

</details>


### [100] [Vanilla Exact Synthesis of CNOT Circuits is NP-hard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04160)
*Chenjian Li,Ji Guan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了最基础的精确CNOT综合问题（恒等输入、固定数量标记量子比特、无辅助比特、全连接CNOT）的判定版本是NP完全的，其优化版本是NP难的。证明通过两步多项式归约实现：首先将网格图等距嵌入超立方体，然后引入"记录器"量子比特将哈密顿路径的中间顶点访问信息编码到最终变换中，从而强制CNOT电路实现既定路径结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多种相关的CNOT综合模型已被证明是计算困难的，但之前的困难性证明都依赖于附加结构（如受限量子比特连通性、编码输入或不受限的中间变量）。最基本的设定——恒等输入、固定数量的标记量子比特、无辅助比特、全连通CNOT——其计算复杂度一直悬而未决。本工作解决了这一开放问题。

Method: 证明了vanilla精确CNOT综合问题的NP完全性。证明给出了从网格图上的哈密顿路径问题到该问题的多项式时间归约，分两步进行：(1) 通过一元编码映射将网格图等距嵌入超立方体；(2) 将这一超立方体哈密顿路径问题编码为vanilla精确CNOT综合问题。核心难点在于CNOT综合仅规定最终奇偶校验矩阵，无法直接强制哈密顿路径所需的中间顶点访问，为此引入了额外的"记录器"量子比特，将必需的中间顶点访问编码进最终变换中。

Result: 证明vanilla精确CNOT综合的判定版本是NP完全的，进而其优化版本是NP难的。该结果还直接蕴含了多个相关问题的困难性，包括GL(n,2)上的最短字问题、GL(n,2)上Cayley图上的距离计算、顺序XOR程序的最小化，以及相位多项式电路的精确综合。

Conclusion: 该结果填补了CNOT综合复杂度领域的一个关键空白，表明即使在最基本的无附加限制的设定下，精确CNOT综合也是计算困难的。同时，由于CNOT综合与线性量子电路和非线性相位多项式电路之间的紧密联系，该结论对更广泛的量子电路综合问题也具有重要意义。

Abstract: Exact CNOT synthesis asks for a minimum-size CNOT circuit implementing an invertible linear transformation. Although several related synthesis models have been shown to be computationally hard, their hardness proofs rely on additional structure such as restricted qubit connectivity, encoded inputs, or unrestricted intermediate variables. The complexity of the most basic setting---identity input, a fixed number of labelled qubits, no ancillas, and all-to-all CNOT connectivity---had remained unresolved. In this work, we prove that the decision version of this vanilla exact CNOT synthesis problem is NP-complete, and consequently that its optimization version is NP-hard. Our proof gives a polynomial-time reduction from the Hamiltonian-path problem on grid graphs in two steps. First, we isometrically embed the grid graph into a hypercube via a unary encoding map. We then encode this hypercube Hamiltonian path problem into vanilla exact CNOT synthesis. The main challenge is that CNOT synthesis specifies only the final parity matrix and cannot directly enforce the intermediate vertex visits required by a Hamiltonian path. To overcome this difficulty, we introduce extra recorder qubits that encode the required intermediate vertex visits into the final transformation, forcing any CNOT circuit implementation to realize the intended path structure. Beyond CNOT synthesis, our result directly implies hardness for several related problems, including the shortest word problem over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, distance computation on Cayley graphs over $\mathrm{GL}(n,2)$, minimization of sequential XOR programs, and exact synthesis of phase polynomial circuits.

</details>


### [101] [Parameterised graph theory for tensor networks: entanglement rerouting, structural simplification, and agnostic tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04165)
*Matthias C. Caro,Natalie McHugh,Sergii Strelchuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用参数化图理论研究了张量网络态（TNS）的表示与层析问题：证明了割宽（cutwidth）和树割宽（tree-cutwidth）限制了TNS压缩为矩阵乘积态（MPS）或树张量网络（TTN）所需的键维开销；提出了新的图参数"学习复杂度"来刻画TNS层析的样本与计算复杂度；并将可实现的TNS学习器扩展到了不可知（agnostic）场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化图理论已在张量网络模拟中证明有用（Markov和Shi, 2008），但其对张量网络表示和层析的影响尚未被充分理解。核心问题是：哪些图参数决定TNS是否存在可处理的MPS或TTN表示，以及哪些图参数控制学习该状态的复杂度。

Method: (1) 通过"纠缠重路由"（entanglement rerouting）技术——经典网络中信息重路由的张量网络类比——证明了割宽和树割宽分别界定TNS表示为MPS和TTN时的键维开销，TTN情形下树割宽还限制分组子系统的局域维。(2) 扩展了(Cramer et al., 2010)的去纠缠MPS学习器（后续由Bakshi等人和Lin等人进一步分析）到TTN及任意已知图上的张量网络，导出了依赖于割宽、树割宽和新参数"学习复杂度"的样本与计算复杂度上界，后者可用度和树宽界定。(3) 将框架扩展到不可知设置：对任意输入态，学习器输出一个纯态，其保真度与给定图和键维下张量网络态的最优值差距在加法误差ε内，并给出了显式的图依赖样本与计算复杂度界。

Result: (1) 建立了割宽→MPS键维开销、树割宽→TTN键维与局域维开销的定量关系。(2) 为任意已知图上的TNS层析提供了图依赖的样本与计算复杂度上界，其中新图参数"学习复杂度"可由度和树宽界定。(3) 在不可知学习设置下，实现了对任意输入态输出ε-最优纯态的算法，并给出显式的复杂度界。

Conclusion: 参数化图理论为张量网络的压缩表示（MPS/TTN）和量子态层析提供了系统的理论框架：图的结构参数（割宽、树割宽）定量决定了表示开销，新引入的学习复杂度刻画了状态学习的本质难度。该工作弥合了张量网络模拟与张量网络表示/层析之间的理论鸿沟，并为更一般的图结构上的量子态学习提供了可操作的算法保障。

Abstract: Parameterised graph theory studies how the complexity of graph-theoretic problems depends on structural parameters of the input graph. This perspective has proved useful in analysing tensor-network simulation (Markov and Shi, 2008). Its implications for tensor-network representations and tomography are less well understood. In particular, which graph parameters determine whether a tensor-network state (TNS) admits a tractable matrix product state (MPS) or tree tensor network (TTN) representation, and which control the complexity of learning the state?
  We address these questions using parameterised graph theory. First, we show that cutwidth and tree-cutwidth bound the bond dimension overhead required to represent a TNS as an MPS or TTN. In the TTN case, tree-cutwidth also bounds the local dimension of the grouped subsystems. The proofs are based on entanglement rerouting, a tensor-network analogue of rerouting information in a classical network. Second, we derive graph-dependent upper bounds on the sample and computational complexity of realisable TNS tomography, with exponents that depend on cutwidth, tree-cutwidth, and a new graph parameter, learning complexity, which we bound in terms of degree and treewidth. We obtain these results by extending the disentangling MPS learner of (Cramer et al., 2010), as analysed further in (Bakshi et al., 2025; Lin et al., 2025), to TTNs and to tensor networks on arbitrary known graphs. Finally, we extend the framework beyond the realisable setting. For an arbitrary input state, our agnostic learner outputs a pure state whose fidelity is within additive error $ε$ of the optimum over tensor-network states on the given graph with a given bond dimension, with explicit graph-dependent bounds on sample and computational complexity.

</details>


### [102] [Measurements on the separated subsystems of an entangled state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04178)
*Gregory D. Scholes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需依赖随机坍缩假设的方法，从自由向量空间的陪集来解析复合量子系统中分离子系统上的测量投影，从而从新的视角解释非局域性的起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合量子系统的纠缠态已被广泛研究，但分离子系统A和B上的测量应与其各自的局部希尔伯特空间中的向量对应。然而，除了可分离态之外，如何在张量积基下解析出分离子系统的相关态并不明确，这一问题长期未得到解决。

Method: 作者证明，分离子系统测量所探测到的任意一般纠缠态的投影，可通过考虑张量积空间定义所源自的自由向量空间中相应的（陪集形式的）态来获得。

Result: 结果表明无需引入随机坍缩假设即可解析分离子系统的测量投影；在此视角下，非局域性源于每个分离子系统上的测量对可能测量结果的投影方式。

Conclusion: 该工作为纠缠态中分离子系统测量提供了新的数学框架，消除了对随机坍缩的依赖，并从测量投影的角度对量子非局域性给出了新的理解。

Abstract: The entangled states of composite quantum systems are well studied. The particle-like nature of these systems also means that, while entangled, they can be physically separated and measurements performed on the separated subsystems. Measurements on the separated subsystems A and B should pertain to vectors in the local Hilbert space of each subsystem, but to date it has not been clear how to elucidate the relevant states of the separated subsystems because it is not obvious how to resolve them from states given in the tensor product basis, except for the separable states. Here it is shown that the projections of any general entangled state that are detected by measurements on the separated subsystems can be obtained considering the corresponding (cosets of) states in the free vector space from which the tensor product space is defined. The result eliminates the need to invoke random collapse, and from this perspective nonlocality arises because of the way measurements on each separated subsystem projects possible measurement outcomes.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum communication and Bell nonlocality require infinite classical communication to simulate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04182)
*Carlos de Gois,Thyago S. R. Santos,Carlos Vieira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文彻底解决了量子维度中经典模拟通信与量子相关性这一核心问题：对于任意维度均给出了结论——qutrit（三维）可用357个经典比特精确模拟，而自ququart（四维）起，任何有限量的经典通信都无法精确模拟，呈现出意料之外的维数四临界转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自量子信息理论建立以来，一个基础而悬而未决的问题是：量子系统状态连续无穷多，却无法传输无限经典信息，这暗示其通信与相关性或许可用有限经典通信模拟。此前仅知qubit（二维）情形需要且只需2个经典比特，数十年来再无进展，作者希望解决所有维度下的这一经典模拟问题。

Method: 作者分别处理两类核心问题：(1)精确模拟任意维度的量子通信；(2)精确模拟两个纠缠量子系统间观测到的全部相关性。对qutrit情形，作者显式构造了一个需要357个经典比特的精确模拟协议，并借助共享随机性实现所有两纠缠qutrit相关性的模拟。对四维及以上情形，通过理论证明给出不可能性结果。

Result: 关键发现：自维度四（ququart）起，即使拥有无限共享随机性，任何有限量的经典通信都无法精确模拟ququart通信以及两个纠缠ququarts的所有量子相关性。这一不可能性转变始于维度四而非预期的维度三，出乎意料。同时在qutrit维度上给出了357个经典比特的显式精确模拟协议。

Conclusion: 本文彻底终结了任意维度下量子通信与量子相关性的经典模拟问题，揭示了一个此前无人预见的定性转变发生在维度四。该结果深刻改变了人们对量子经典界面的理解，也为量子模拟与量子信息理论的后续研究提供了新的基石。

Abstract: A quantum system of any fixed dimension can be prepared in a continuum of states, yet it cannot be used to transmit an unlimited amount of classical information. Similarly, the correlations observed between measurement outcomes on separate parts of a shared quantum system can be stronger than classical correlations, but they cannot transmit information. These fundamental limitations suggest that the statistics observed from quantum communication and quantum correlations may admit a simulation using a finite amount of classical communication. This expectation is confirmed in the smallest nontrivial quantum dimension, with two classical bits being necessary and sufficient to exactly simulate qubit communication and all correlations between qubits. Despite significant efforts during the previous decades, this remained the only solved case. Here we resolve both problems for every quantum dimension. The solution reveals an unexpected qualitative transition starting at dimension four: no finite amount of classical communication can exactly simulate ququart communication nor all quantum correlations of two entangled ququarts, even with unlimited shared randomness. One might have expected this transition, if it existed, to appear already for qutrits. Instead, we construct an explicit protocol that exactly simulates qutrit communication using $357$ classical bits, and consequently, all correlations of two entangled qutrits.

</details>

### [104] [Valley polarization dependence of candidate even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states in monolayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02976)
*Saswata Sahu,Amarendra Pr. Indra,Moumita Indra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层石墨烯中偶分母分数量子霍尔（EDFQH）态，聚焦于实验观测到的ν=1/2和ν=1/4填充因子。利用SU(4)对称性导致的复合费米子框架构建了不同自旋、谷和混合极化构型的变分波函数，并通过球面几何中库仑相互作用数值比较了各极化扇区的基态能量，确定能量有利的候选态为多分量关联量子霍尔液体，而非简单的复合费米子费米海描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶分母分数量子霍尔态是研究非阿贝尔统计和可能拓扑量子计算的候选平台，但在传统半导体系统中较为罕见。石墨烯由于自旋和谷自由度带来的近似SU(4)对称性，为稳定此类态提供了更丰富的可能性。理解其物理机制对于探索新奇的量子物态具有重要意义。

Method: 采用Chern-Simons复合费米子框架处理SU(4)系统，构建对应不同自旋、谷及混合极化构型的候选变分波函数。在球面几何中利用库仑相互作用对各类候选态的基态能量进行数值计算，并通过跨极化扇区的能量比较来确定能量上有利的候选态。

Result: 能量比较结果表明，在ν=1/2和ν=1/4处能量上有利的候选态对应于多分量关联量子霍尔液体，且在所考虑的限制性试探态族内，这些态与简单的复合费米子费米海描述存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了谷极化和SU(4)对称性在稳定石墨烯偶分母分数量子霍尔态中的关键作用，为理解这些新奇量子态的形成机制提供了理论见解，并指出需要超越简单复合费米子费米海图像来描述这些体系。

Abstract: We investigate even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (EDFQH) states in monolayer graphene, focusing on the experimentally observed filling fractions $ν= 1/2$ and $ν= 1/4$. Owing to the approximate SU(4) symmetry arising from spin and valley degrees of freedom, graphene hosts a rich variety of multi-component fractional quantum Hall states with different polarization configurations. Using the Chern-Simons composite fermion framework for SU(4) systems, we construct candidate variational wave functions corresponding to distinct spin, valley, and mixed polarization states. The ground-state energies of these states are evaluated numerically using the Coulomb interaction in spherical geometry. By comparing energies across different polarization sectors, we identify the energetically favored configurations at $ν= 1/2$ and $ν= 1/4$. Our variational energy comparison suggests that the energetically favored candidate states correspond to multi-component correlated quantum Hall liquids. Within the restricted family of trial states considered here, these states differ from a simple composite-fermion Fermi-sea description. These findings provide insight into the role of valley polarization and SU(4) symmetry in stabilizing even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states in graphene.

</details>


### [105] [Orbital current rectifier and linear magnon Edelstein effect in $p$-wave antialtermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03075)
*Kristian Mæland,Björn Trauzettel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文证明了p波磁体通过其磁振子的轨道磁矩产生线性的热Edelstein效应，从而实现高效磁化；同时它们能通过纯粹的偶数阶非线性响应产生完全非互易的轨道电流，可用于轨道电流整流。这些输运现象源于磁振子的轨道磁矩，将磁振子学与轨道电子学联系起来。更一般地，奇宇称波磁体中共面基态所承载的磁振子具有零自旋磁矩，但携带共线面外轨道磁矩，实现了轨道版本的"反交错磁"（antialtermagnetism）。结论在有无自旋-轨道耦合的情况下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索p波磁体中由磁振子轨道磁矩驱动的热电输运现象，建立磁振子学与轨道电子学之间的联系，并寻找实现轨道电流整流的新型材料平台。

Method: 采用对称性分析建立一般规则，并以CeNiAsO基态启发的最小亚晶格数共面磁结构作为具体模型，通过明确计算进行验证。同时考察了存在和不存在自旋-轨道耦合两种情况。

Result: 发现p波磁体中磁振子携带共线的面外轨道磁矩（而非自旋磁矩），可通过线性热Edelstein效应产生磁化；偶数阶非线性响应可产生完全非互易的轨道电流，实现轨道整流效应，且不受自旋-轨道耦合影响。

Conclusion: p波磁体是一类有前景的轨道电流整流候选材料，其磁振子承载轨道磁矩这一特性实现了磁振子学与轨道电子学的交叉融合，并提出了"轨道反交错磁"这一新概念。

Abstract: We show that $p$-wave magnets efficiently generate magnetization via a linear thermal Edelstein effect arising from the orbital magnetic moments of their magnons. Furthermore, they can generate perfectly nonreciprocal orbital currents through a purely even-order nonlinear response. This makes them promising candidates for orbital-current rectification. Because these transport phenomena originate from the orbital magnetic moment of magnons, they connect magnonics and orbitronics. More generally, we find that odd-parity-wave magnets with coplanar ground states host magnons with zero spin magnetic moment. Instead the magnons carry a collinear, out-of-plane orbital magnetic moment, realizing an orbital version of antialtermagnetism. We establish these general results using symmetry arguments and demonstrate them explicitly for a coplanar ground state with the minimal number of sublattices, inspired by the ground state of CeNiAsO. Our conclusions hold both in absence and presence of spin-orbit coupling.

</details>


### [106] [Antiferromagnetism-altered plasmon dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03138)
*Yifan Su,Suheng Xu,Rocco A. Vitalone,Ziyu Liu,Emil Viñas Boström,Na Wu,Chun-Ying Huang,Zhuquan Zhang,Jaehoon Jung,Daniel G. Chica,Vinícius da Silveira Lan Avelar,Yiping Wang,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James C. Hone,Xiaoyang Zhu,Cory R. Dean,Xavier Roy,Angel Rubio,D. N. Basov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文首次在实验上实现了基于二维材料石墨烯/NiPS₃界面的等离激元-磁振子混合平台。通过散射型扫描近场光学显微镜（s-SNOT）结合太赫兹辐射，观察到等离激元极化激元的色散关系在NiPS₃反铁磁转变处的重正化，并提供等离激元与反铁磁序耦合的坚实证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离激元与磁振子之间的相互作用是凝聚态物理与自旋电子学中长期追寻的现象。在三维体系中，能量尺度失配抑制了这种耦合；而二维等离激元具有无隙色散，可在宽光谱范围内与磁振子重叠。尽管有大量理论预测，实验上始终未能观察到磁振子-等离激元相互作用，因此需要实验验证平台的实现。

Method: 构建石墨烯/NiPS₃异质结构作为混合等离子-磁振子平台，部署散射型扫描近场光学显微镜（s-SNOM）搭配太赫兹辐射，在石墨烯/NiPS₃界面成像传播的等离激元波包，并追踪其跨NiPS₃反铁磁转变的动力学行为。同时结合互补的拉曼散射与纳米太赫兹光谱来分析谱权重再分配和介电屏蔽变化。

Result: 观察到等离激元极化激元色散在NiPS₃反铁磁序出现时发生清晰的再正常化。互补光谱揭示了可能与多磁振子连续谱相关的谱权重再分配与介电屏蔽变化，为等离激元与反铁磁序的耦合提供了直接实验证据。

Conclusion: 该工作为二维材料中混合磁振子-等离激元相互作用提供了首个实验平台，是这一领域的重要里程碑，为相干自旋-等离激元器件和可调太赫兹自旋电子学组件的实现开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The interaction between plasmons and magnons is a long-sought phenomenon with implications for fundamental physics and spintronics applications. In three-dimensional systems, this coupling is suppressed by the large mismatch in energy scales, but two-dimensional (2D) plasmons with gapless dispersion can overlap with magnons over a broad spectral range. Despite numerous theoretical predictions, experimental observation of magnon-plasmon interaction has remained elusive. In this work, we study a first-of-its-kind hybrid plasmon-magnon platform based on 2D materials. By deploying scattering-type scanning near-field optical microscopy (s-SNOM) with terahertz radiation, we image propagating plasmon wavepackets at a graphene/NiPS$_3$ interface and track their dynamics across the antiferromagnetic transition of NiPS$_3$. We observe a clear renormalization of the plasmon-polariton dispersion concurrent with the onset of antiferromagnetic order. With complementary Raman scattering and nano-terahertz spectroscopy, we unveil spectral weight redistribution and dielectric screening changes, potentially associated with the multi-magnon continuum, as the underlying mechanism. These results provide solid evidence of coupling between plasmon and antiferromagnetic order, marking a cornerstone for a potential platform for hybrid magnon-plasmon interactions in 2D materials, opening avenues for coherent spin-plasmon devices and tunable terahertz spintronic components.

</details>


### [107] [Moiré-induced altermagnetism from nonmagnetic constituents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03271)
*Jingtian Shi,Maxim Khodas,Ivar Martin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个非磁性材料通过与非磁性衬底的莫尔干涉产生交变磁性（altermagnetic）序的机制。该机制由结构弛豫和晶格失配的莫尔方格中莫尔畴壁的自发扭转驱动。当每个莫尔畴掺入一个电子时，电子相互作用会打开关联绝缘隙。根据莫尔势极小值的位置，莫尔能带可呈现d波或g波的交变磁劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非磁性材料中通过莫尔干涉产生交变磁性（altermagnetic）这一新型磁性有序态的机制，拓宽交变磁性的材料实现途径，而不依赖于材料的固有磁性。

Method: 利用晶格失配的莫尔方晶格结构，通过结构弛豫效应与莫尔畴壁中的自发扭转（twirls）来实现交变磁性序；结合一个电子/莫尔畴的掺杂条件，通过电子相互作用打开关联绝缘隙，并分析莫尔势极小值位置对d波或g波交变磁劈裂的影响。

Result: 在特定掺杂条件下打开关联绝缘隙；莫尔能带可呈现d波或g波交变磁劈裂，其中d波劈裂可通过有限转角增强，而g波劈裂对应变敏感，应变可驱动其向d波劈裂转变。

Conclusion: 莫尔超结构提供了一种无需材料本征磁性即可实现交变磁性序的新方法，且通过转角与应变等参数可调控交变磁劈裂的对称性（d波或g波），为交变磁性材料的工程化设计提供了崭新途径。

Abstract: We propose a mechanism for nonmagnetic materials to develop altermagnetic order by moiré interference with nonmagnetic substrate, which is driven by structural relaxation and spontaneous twirls in moiré domain walls of lattice-mismatched moiré square lattices. When doped with one electron per moiré domain, a correlated insulating gap is opened by electron interaction. Depending on the location of the moiré potential minima, the moiré bands can show d-wave or g-wave altermagnetic splitting. The former can be enhanced by a finite twist angle; the latter is sensitive to strains that drive a transition to d-wave.

</details>


### [108] [Resolving the Magnetic Ground-State Controversy in RuO2 through A Flat Magnetic Energy Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03274)
*Tianxiao Liang,Fanhan Kong,Jijun Zhao,Xue Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文针对金红石RuO2的磁基态争议（非磁态vs交错磁态），开发了一个广义的、依赖环境的自旋-晶格框架，统一了局域Heisenberg交换、巡游Stoner磁性（通过Landau自旋涨落）和自旋-轨道耦合介导的自旋-晶格相互作用。该框架由机器学习参数化的高通量第一性原理数据库驱动，并通过大规模蒙特卡洛模拟求解，揭示RuO2存在异常平坦的磁能量景观，其中非磁态与多种交错磁构型近简并。材料扰动（如本征缺陷）主要通过修改局域Heisenberg交换来选择不同的磁基态，Stoner极化提供次要贡献，而自旋-轨道耦合仅控制Néel矢量的取向和稳定性，不决定长程磁有序的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RuO2是交错磁（altermagnetism）的重要候选材料，但实验对其磁基态的报道相互矛盾——有实验报告非磁态，有实验报告交错磁有序。这一争议亟需一个统一的框架来解释，理解材料扰动如何在不同竞争磁基态之间做出选择，对于交错磁材料的研究和应用至关重要。

Method: 作者开发了一个广义的环境依赖自旋-晶格框架，整合了三类关键物理机制：(1) 局域Heisenberg交换相互作用；(2) 通过Landau自旋涨落描述的巡游Stoner磁性；(3) 自旋-轨道耦合介导的自旋-晶格相互作用。该框架通过机器学习从高通量第一性原理数据库中参数化，并使用大规模蒙特卡洛模拟求解，同时系统研究了本征缺陷等材料扰动对磁基态选择的影响。

Result: 框架揭示了RuO2具有异常平坦的磁能量景观，非磁态与多种交错磁构型近乎简并。材料扰动（以本征缺陷为例）主要通过修改局域Heisenberg交换来选择不同的磁基态，扰动诱导的巡游Stoner极化提供重要的次要贡献。自旋-轨道耦合控制Néel矢量的取向和稳定性，但并不决定长程磁有序是否出现。

Conclusion: 该研究为相互矛盾的实验观察提供了统一的解释，并建立了一个通用的微观框架，用于理解在具有近平坦磁能量景观的系统中，材料扰动如何选择竞争的磁基态。这一框架不仅解答了RuO2的磁基态争议，也为其他存在多态简并的磁性材料研究提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: Rutile RuO2 is a prominent candidate for altermagnetism, yet its magnetic ground state remains highly controversial, with experiments reporting either a nonmagnetic state or altermagnetic order. Here, we develop a generalized environment-dependent spin-lattice framework that unifies localized Heisenberg exchange, itinerant Stoner magnetism via Landau spin fluctuations, and spin-orbit-coupling-mediated spin-lattice interactions. Parameterized from a high-throughput first-principles database using machine-learning and solved by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations, the framework reveals an exceptionally flat magnetic energy landscape in RuO2, where the nonmagnetic state lies nearly degenerate with multiple altermagnetic configurations. We find that material perturbations, exemplified by intrinsic defects, select distinct magnetic ground states primarily by modifying the localized Heisenberg exchange, with perturbation-induced itinerant Stoner polarization provides an essential secondary contribution. Spin-orbit coupling controls the orientation and stability of the Néel vector, but does not determine the emergence of long-range magnetic order. These results provide a unified explanation for the conflicting experimental observations and establish a general microscopic framework for understanding how material perturbations select competing magnetic ground states in systems with nearly flat magnetic energy landscapes.

</details>


### [109] [Symmetry-breaking line defects embedded to a 3D $O(N)$ critical bulk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03289)
*Shuai Yang,Liang-dong Hu,Yan Chen,W. Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过模糊球面（fuzzy sphere）技术研究嵌入3D O(N) Wilson-Fisher临界环境中的线缺陷（钉扎场缺陷线）。作者发现针对N≳3存在稳定的缺陷共形固定点（重整化群稳定、无相关算符、有效自由度低于体固定点），且缺陷域壁算符在N≳3时变得无关，而在N<3时为相关。这表明N<3时因域壁增殖无法维持自发对称性破缺的缺陷固定点，而N≳3时对称性破缺缺陷可以实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维经典短程相互作用系统无法自发破缺离散对称性，但在嵌入临界环境中的线缺陷上，这种对称性破缺是否可能稳定存在是一个开放问题。作者希望通过研究缺陷与体临界性的耦合来解答这一问题，为低维缺陷与高维体临界耦合的普适行为提供新见解。

Method: 采用模糊球面（fuzzy sphere）技术，构建3D O(N) Wilson-Fisher临界体中的钉扎场线缺陷；通过计算g函数比较缺陷固定点与体固定点的有效自由度；分析各种N下的缺陷域壁（domain wall）算符的标度维度，以判断其相关性与该缺陷孤立的O(2)类对称性破缺相与N=1依赖的O(N)普适类的行为。

Result: 提供了缺陷共形固定点稳定存在的可信证据（无相关算符、g函数更小）。域壁算符在N≳3时无关而在N<3时相关。综合表明N<3时域壁增殖导致无法形成稳定的对称性自发破缺缺陷固定点，而N≳3时对称性破缺缺陷固定点是可能的。

Conclusion: 嵌入临界体的1D缺陷能否支持离散对称性自发破缺取决于N：在O(N) Wilson-Fisher普适类中，N<3时域壁增殖抑制了对称性破缺缺陷的稳定性；N≳3时则存在稳定的缺陷共形固定点，缺陷上的离散对称性可以自发破缺。

Abstract: While spontaneous breaking of a discrete symmetry in one-dimensional classical systems with short-range interactions is absent, it is expected that a line defect embedded in a bulk criticality exhibits a stable discrete symmetry spontaneous breaking. Here, we investigate the behavior of a pinning-field line defect immersed in a 3D bulk that remains tuned to the $O(N)$ Wilson-Fisher critical point. Employing the fuzzy sphere technique, we provide convincing evidence of the existence of stable defect conformal fixed points, and we demonstrate their renormalization group stability by showing no relevant operator and less effective degrees of freedom than that at bulk fixed point via $g$-function. Moreover, we investigate the defect domain wall operator for various $N$, and we identify that it becomes irrelevance for $N\gtrsim 3$ but it is relevant for $N<3$.These evidence indicate that a one-dimensional defect coupled to a critical bulk cannot support a stable symmetry spontaneously broken defect fixed point due to domain wall proliferation for $N<3$ Wilson-Fisher universality, while in the case of $N \gtrsim 3$ a symmetry broken defect is possible.

</details>


### [110] [Studying line defect at Deconfined Quantum Criticality via fuzzy sphere regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03465)
*Shutao Liu,Shuai Yang,Jie Lou,Yan Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用模糊球面正则化，数值研究了(2+1)维去禁闭量子临界体与(0+1)维钉扎场缺陷的耦合系统，首次给出了去禁闭量子临界点上线缺陷的共形数据（包括缺陷改变/产生算符的标度维数和缺陷g函数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体临界涨落与非平凡拓扑的相互作用可以丰富缺陷物理并产生新的缺陷普适类，理解此类缺陷的命运是一个重要的开放问题，但目前缺乏数值表征。

Method: 采用模糊球面正则化方法，数值求解(2+1)维去禁闭量子临界体与(0+1)维钉扎场缺陷耦合的系统，通过测量缺陷算符谱提取相关普适量。

Result: 提取了缺陷共形不动点的多个普适量，包括缺陷改变(产生)算符的标度维数和缺陷g函数，建立了去禁闭量子临界点线缺陷共形数据的首次数值表征。

Conclusion: 该结果为去禁闭量子临界点处的缺陷临界现象提供了第一手数值证据，可能激发拓扑量子临界物质中缺陷临界现象的进一步研究。

Abstract: The interplay between bulk critical fluctuations and nontrivial topology can enrich defect physics and give rise to novel defect universality classes. Understanding the fate of such defects therefore constitutes an important open problem. In this work, we studied a particularly simple setting: a (0+1)-dimensional pinning-field defect coupled to a (2+1)-dimensional deconfined quantum critical bulk. Using the fuzzy-sphere regularization, we numerically investigated the defect operator spectrum and extracted several universal quantities characterizing the defect conformal fixed point, including the scaling dimensions of defect-changing(creating) operators and the defect \(g\)-function. These results establish the first numerical characterization of line-defect conformal data at deconfined quantum criticality and may stimulate further investigations of defect critical phenomena in topological quantum critical matter.

</details>


### [111] [Such stuff as magic is made on: compact operator algebra, stabilizer polytope and the structure of reduced density matrices in a Kitaev spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03485)
*Snigdh Sabharwal,Nic Shannon,Paul Skrzypczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子多体系统中"魔力"（magic）这一非稳定子资源在局部子系统中的涌现机制，以Kitaev蜂巢模型为对象，揭示了魔力涌现温度以下生成的紧致算子空间结构，这些算子空间可由局域边界的对称性独立导出（在六位六边形末梢情形下形成对称分辨的plaquette代数），并具有有限维欧几里得Jordan代数的代数结构。该约化描述不仅适用于魔力，还可推广到其他量子资源（如真多体纠缠），为理解有限温度Kitaev自旋液体及多体系统量子资源的约化描述开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统展现丰富复杂的物理行为，魔力（magic）作为新型诊断工具可补充多体纠缠等其他关键特征。作者希望探究魔力如何在更大量子多体系统的子系统中涌现，并通过其涌现机制揭示底层多体物理中的重要结构。

Method: 以Kitaev蜂巢模型为核心研究对象：(1) 识别局部子系统获得魔力的临界温度；(2) 在魔力涌现临界点分析最优魔力见证者，提取承载局域热态的紧致算子空间；(3) 在六位六边形末梢情形下，从局域边界的对称性独立推导相同的算子空间，构造对称分辨的plaquette代数；(4) 分析这些算子空间的代数结构，识别其有限维欧几里得Jordan代数形式，并用键算符和键环算符给出自然物理解释；(5) 将该约化描述推广到其他量子资源（如真多体纠缠）。

Result: 发现存在明确的温度阈值，低于该阈值局部子系统获得非零魔力。魔力涌现临界点上最优魔力见证者揭示的紧致算子空间能够在之后的整个热演化过程中持续捕获局域热态。在六位六边形情形下，该算子空间可由局域边界的对称性独立推导，当对称性被精确实现时，局域态完全位于该代数内，约化magic robustness等于完全magic robustness。这些算子空间具有有限维欧几里得Jordan代数的丰富代数结构，可在键与键环算符框架下获得自然理解。该方法亦可推广至真多体纠缠等其他局域量子资源。

Conclusion: 本文表明，局域魔力的涌现可以揭示有限温度Kitaev自旋液体中紧致且具有物理意义的算子结构，为多体系统中量子资源的约化描述开辟了新途径。魔力作为诊断量子多体系统中隐藏算子结构的工具具有普适潜力，且所揭示的算子代数结构可能广泛适用于多种量子资源的研究。

Abstract: Quantum many-body systems exhibit rich and complex behaviour. Magic has recently emerged as a powerful new diagnostic for probing such systems, complementing other key features such as many-body entanglement. Here, starting from an investigation of the onset of magic within subsystems of a larger many-body quantum system, we show that intricate structures emerge which shed important light on the underlying many-body physics. Focusing on the Kitaev honeycomb model, we first identify the temperature below which local subsystems acquire magic. Remarkably, we find that the optimal magic witnesses active at the onset of magic reveal compact operator spaces that continue to capture the local thermal states throughout their subsequent evolution. For the six-site hexagonal marginal, this connection can be made stronger: the same operator space emerges independently from the symmetries of the local marginal and forms the symmetry-resolved plaquette algebra. When these symmetries are realized exactly, the local state lies entirely within this algebra and the reduced robustness of magic is equal to the full robustness of magic. The same reduced description can also be applied to other local quantum resources, which we illustrate using genuine multipartite entanglement. Furthermore, the operator spaces possess a rich algebraic structure in the form of finite-dimensional Euclidean Jordan algebras, with a natural interpretation in terms of bond and bond-cycle operators. Our general methodology therefore show how the onset of local magic can reveal a compact, physically meaningful operator structure underlying the finite-temperature Kitaev spin liquid, and opens up a new avenue towards reduced descriptions of quantum resources in many-body systems.

</details>


### [112] [Competing lattice structures induced by Sn substitution in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03513)
*Anshu Kataria,Ilija K. Nikolov,Armando Consiglio,Giuseppe Allodi,Ginevra Corsale,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Domenico Di Sante,Vesna F. Mitrović,Samuele Sanna,Pietro Bonfà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过121Sb核四极共振（NQR）测量和密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，研究了CsV3Sb5-xSnx中Sn取代对kagome金属竞争相的影响，揭示了Sn取代诱导的局部结构畸变与竞争结构不稳定性之间的复杂相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解化学取代对kagome金属竞争相的影响对于厘清局部结构畸变与电子不稳定性之间的相互作用至关重要。在CsV3Sb5中，Sn取代显著改变了电子相图，但其微观机制尚不清楚。

Method: 结合121Sb核四极共振（NQR）测量和密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，系统研究CsV3Sb5-xSnx中Sn取代的原子尺度效应。

Result: 低Sn浓度下观测到与Sn取代诱导的局部结构畸变一致的卫星NQR峰信号；这些杂质诱导的特征在高达x=0.65的整个实验掺杂范围内持续存在，且直至室温仍可观测。对于全掺杂理想情形x=1，计算表明kagome晶格的动力学不稳定性促使两个能量近乎简并的平衡结构稳定化，其特征为V三聚体，并沿c轴方向相邻层间存在零或π相位差。

Conclusion: Sn取代驱动了钒基kagome化合物中局部杂质诱导畸变与竞争结构不稳定性之间的复杂相互作用。

Abstract: Understanding the effect of chemical substitution on competing phases of kagome metals is crucial for disentangling the interplay between local structural distortions and electronic instabilities. In \cvs, Sn substitution strongly modifies the electronic phase diagram, yet the microscopic mechanism driving this remains unclear. Here, we combine $^{121}$Sb nuclear quadrupole resonance (NQR) measurements and density functional theory calculations to investigate the atomic-scale effects of Sn substitution in CsV$_3$Sb$_{5-x}$Sn$_x$. At low Sn concentrations, the observed satellite NQR peaks exhibit signatures of local structural distortion induced by Sn substitution, qualitatively consistent with our computational analysis. These impurity-induced features persist across the entire experimentally investigated doping range, up to $x$ = 0.65, and remain observable up to room temperature. For $x=1$, the fully doped idealized case, the estimated dynamical instabilities of the kagome lattice suggest the stabilization of two nearly energy-degenerate equilibrium structures characterized by V-trimers, distinguished by a zero- or $π$-phase shift between adjacent layers along the $c$-axis. Together, these results show that Sn substitution drives a complex interplay between local impurity-induced distortions and competing structural instabilities in vanadium-based kagome compounds.

</details>


### [113] [Non-Resonant Impulsively Stimulated Raman Scattering by a Terahertz Field: a Case Study of 1T-TaS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03519)
*Haotian Zhang,Yuheng Guo,Zidu Yu,Yongbo Lv,Yiting Wang,Liwen Feng,Jiaying Xu,Tianlong Xia,Xinbo Wang,Hao Chu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了1T-TaS2中拉曼活性声子与太赫兹电场耦合的机制，证明改进的受激拉曼散射（涉及双光子吸收/非共振拉曼散射）是这些集体模式被相干激发和观测的物理基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹超快及非线性光谱技术已广泛应用于凝聚态系统的集体激发研究，但在中心对称系统中，Raman活性集体模式不直接与太赫兹电场耦合，其光-物质相互作用的实现机制此前未被明确讨论。

Method: 对1T-TaS2（具有公度、近公度与非公度电荷密度波相的材料）开展太赫兹泵浦-光学探测和太赫兹三次谐波产生实验，比较宽带单周期与窄带多周期太赫兹场对拉曼活性声子的激发效果。

Result: CDW相变在动力学拉曼响应中留下清晰特征；结果证明改进的受激拉曼散射机制（涉及双光子吸收，即非共振拉曼散射）是相干激发与观测晶格模式的潜在物理机制。

Conclusion: 该发现对利用低能太赫兹场对集体模式开展光谱研究、相干调控以及固体腔电动力学修饰具有重要参考意义。

Abstract: Time-domain ultrafast and nonlinear terahertz spectroscopy techniques are recently applied to many condensed matter systems for investigating their collective excitations. In centrosymmetric systems, these collective modes are typically Raman-active and therefore do not couple directly to the terahertz electric field. The mechanism by which light-matter interaction realizes in these studies has not been explicitly discussed in detail. In this work, we perform terahertz pump - optical probe and terahertz third harmonic generation investigations on 1T-TaS2, a material exhibiting a rich charge-density-wave (CDW) phase diagram including the commensurate, nearly-commensurate and incommensurate CDW phases. The transition between these distinct states leaves a clear signature on the dynamical Raman response. We investigate how the Raman-active phonons couple to a broadband monocycle terahertz field as well as a narrowband multicycle terahertz field. Our results indicate that a modified impulsively stimulated Raman scattering mechanism involving two-photon absorption, also known as non-resonant Raman scattering, underlies the coherent excitation and observation of the lattice modes. These results are relevant for future spectroscopy investigation and coherent control of collective modes using low-energy terahertz field as well as cavity electrodynamical dressing of solids.

</details>


### [114] [Hidden Magnetic Octupolar Order driven by Spin-Orbit coupling in KFeF$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03684)
*C. A. Crawford,C. I. Hiley,N. C. Bristowe,C. Stock,J. Gainza,C. Ritter,M. R. Lees,R. I. Walton,M. S. Senn*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过对3d钙钛矿KFeF3的高分辨率粉末衍射、磁测量、对称性分析和第一性原理计算，揭示了该材料中一个40 K处由隐藏的磁八极序参量驱动的结构相变，该相变无传统Jahn-Teller畸变或平移对称破缺，且自旋轨道耦合与轨道简并之间的竞争是隐藏多极序出现的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的相变通常由对称性破缺的结构畸变驱动，但某些相变可能由无常规结构特征的隐藏序（hidden order）驱动。作者希望寻找并理解这类隐藏序，特别是探究自旋轨道耦合与轨道简并竞争如何引发高秩磁性多极自由度。

Method: 结合高分辨率粉末衍射、磁强计测量、基于对称性的Landau分析和第一性原理DFT计算，对KFeF3的90 K立方-菱方相变和40 K菱方-单斜相变进行系统表征与解析。

Result: 发现90 K相变源于伴随反铁磁序的常规磁致伸缩；而40 K相变使对称性降至单斜，但无Jahn-Teller畸变或平移对称破缺。Landau分析表明该相变由隐藏的、以z为中心的磁八极序参量驱动，其非适耦合解释了弱铁磁性和显著的对称破缺应变。DFT计算揭示自旋轨道耦合抑制了竞争的Jahn-Teller不稳定性，从而有利于高秩磁性多极自由度。

Conclusion: KFeF3被确立为研究自旋轨道耦合与轨道简并竞争导致隐藏多极序的模型体系，且该类磁性多极序的控制可能为诱导交错磁性（altermagnetism）提供途径。

Abstract: Phase transitions are typically driven by symmetry-breaking structural distortions that lift electronic degeneracies, yet in some cases, these transitions may be driven by a hidden order without conventional structural signatures. Here, we demonstrate that the 3$d$ perovskite, KFeF$_3$, hosts such a hidden instability. Using a combination of high-resolution powder diffraction, magnetometry, symmetry-based analysis and first principles calculations, we reveal that while the 90 K cubic to rhombohedral transition arises from conventional magnetostriction accompanying antiferromagnetic order, a second transition at 40 K lowers the symmetry to monoclinic without any detectable Jahn-Teller distortion or translational symmetry-breaking. Symmetry-based Landau analysis supports a hidden zone centered magnetic octupole order parameter, whose improper coupling accounts for the weak ferromagnetism and pronounced symmetry-breaking strain. Density functional calculations show that spin-orbit coupling suppresses the competing Jahn-Teller instability, and thereby favors higher rank magnetic multipolar degrees of freedom. These findings establish KFeF$_3$ as a model system in which the competing energy scales between spin-orbit coupling and orbital degeneracy result in the emergence of hidden, multipolar order. We show that the control of such magnetic, multipolar order could, in principle, provide a route to induce altermagnetism.

</details>


### [115] [Fermi-Point Topology Determines Emergent Conformal Criticality in Extended Quantum Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03708)
*Mohammad Abbasi,Saeed Mahdavifar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在具有竞争性团簇相互作用、交换各向异性和横向磁场的扩展量子自旋链中，揭示了费米点拓扑、共形临界性与量子纠缠之间的直接对应关系。研究发现Lifshitz转变可产生有效中心荷为1/2、1、3/2、2和3的共形临界相，并指出中心荷并非简单地由晶格能隙闭合点或费米点数目决定，而是由计入格点对称性、倒易格点等价和模式等价后的独立低能连续场扇区数目及共形内容决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维量子系统的量子临界性由涌现共形场论（CFT）刻画，其中中心荷计数独立无间隙自由度。但在微观层面将这种普适共形结构与底层准粒子谱的动量空间拓扑联系起来仍然具有挑战性。本文旨在建立连接费米点拓扑与涌现CFT的微观框架。

Method: 研究一个包含竞争性团簇相互作用、交换各向异性和横向磁场的扩展量子自旋链。通过分析相互作用和磁场驱动的Lifshitz转变来识别共形临界相，并利用实空间和动量空间的纠缠谱作为互补的微观探针，以揭示临界模式的共形内容和动量空间组织方式。

Result: 发现Lifshitz转变可产生有效中心荷c_eff为1/2、1、3/2、2和3的共形临界相，包括一个Ising和Luttinger液体扇区共存的多临界点。关键发现是中心荷不由格点能隙闭合数或费米点数直接决定，而是由计入格点对称性、倒易格点识别和模式等价后的独立低能连续场扇区数决定。此外，谱重构是否产生新的独立连续场扇区决定了Lifshitz转变是否改变中心荷。

Conclusion: 本文建立了将费米点拓扑连接到涌现CFT的微观框架，表明相互作用驱动的谱重构可以产生更高中心荷的临界性和非常规多临界行为，为理解一维量子临界性的拓扑起源提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Quantum criticality in one-dimensional quantum systems is characterized by emergent conformal field theories (CFTs), whose central charge counts independent gapless degrees of freedom. Establishing a microscopic connection between this universal conformal structure and the momentum-space topology of the underlying quasiparticle spectrum remains challenging. Here, we uncover a direct correspondence between Fermi-point topology, conformal criticality, and quantum entanglement in an extended quantum spin chain with competing cluster interactions, exchange anisotropy, and a transverse magnetic field. We show that interaction- and field-driven Lifshitz transitions generate conformal critical phases with effective central charges $c_{\rm eff}=1/2$, $1$, $3/2$, $2$, and $3$, including a multicritical point where Ising and Luttinger-liquid sectors coexist. Importantly, the central charge is not determined simply by the number of lattice gap closings or Fermi points, but by the number and conformal content of independent low-energy continuum sectors after accounting for lattice symmetries, reciprocal-lattice identifications, and mode equivalences. Thus, Lifshitz transitions may leave the conformal anomaly unchanged or modify the central charge depending on whether spectral reconstruction generates new independent continuum sectors. Real- and momentum-space entanglement spectra provide complementary microscopic signatures, revealing both the conformal content and momentum-space organization of critical modes. Our results establish a microscopic framework linking Fermi-point topology to emergent CFTs and show how interaction-driven spectral reconstruction can generate higher-central-charge criticality and unconventional multicritical behavior.

</details>


### [116] [Ground-State Phase Diagram, Higher-Winding Topology, and Lifshitz Criticality in an Anisotropic Four-Spin XX Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03766)
*Mohammad Abbasi,Faraz Valeh,Saeed Mahdavifar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了横向磁场中具有各向异性四自旋簇相互作用的自旋1/2 XX链的量子临界与拓扑性质，发现多个有能隙拓扑相和不同的量子临界边界，包括Lifshitz型转变、非传统多重临界点，以及具有量子化绕数ν=0, ±1, -2, ±3的拓扑相，其中ν=±3的高绕数相展现出比短程Kitaev型链更丰富的拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究各向异性多自旋相互作用如何在低维量子系统中实现更高绕数的拓扑相和非传统多重临界性，为丰富一维量子系统的拓扑物态提供新的调控途径。

Method: 通过研究自旋1/2 XX链在各向异性四自旋簇相互作用和横向磁场下的模型，分析交换各向异性、簇相互作用与外场的竞争效应，考察低能费米点结构的重构、动量空间拓扑的奇异重组，并利用绕数与纠缠谱简并模式来表征不同的拓扑相。

Result: 得到了包含多个有能隙拓扑相和不同量子临界边界的丰富相图；识别出多种由低能费米点重构导致的Lifshitz型转变，包括一个非传统多重临界点；在各向同性极限下出现常规Lifshitz转变。绕数量子化为ν=0, ±1, -2, ±3，其中ν=±3的高绕数相由扩展簇相互作用诱导，比传统短程Kitaev型链拓扑结构更丰富。拓扑区分进一步反映在体纠缠谱最低能级的简并模式上。

Conclusion: 研究证明各向异性多自旋相互作用为在一维量子系统中实现更高绕数的拓扑相和非传统多重临界性提供了一条多样化的途径。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum critical and topological properties of a spin-$1/2$ XX chain with anisotropic four-spin cluster interactions in a transverse magnetic field. The interplay of exchange anisotropy, cluster interactions, and the external field produces a rich phase diagram with multiple gapped topological phases and distinct quantum critical boundaries. We identify several Lifshitz-type transitions arising from reconstructions of the low-energy Fermi-point structure, including an unconventional multicritical point where distinct critical branches intersect and the momentum-space topology undergoes a singular reorganization. In the isotropic limit, a conventional Lifshitz transition emerges through the merging and annihilation of Fermi points, revealing distinct mechanisms of Fermi-point reconstruction within the same model. The gapped phases are characterized by quantized winding numbers $ν=0,\pm1,-2,$ and $\pm3$, with the higher-winding phases induced by the extended cluster interactions. The $ν=\pm3$ phases exhibit a richer topological structure than conventional short-range Kitaev-type chains and are separated from other sectors by field- and interaction-driven gap closings. These topological distinctions are further reflected in characteristic degeneracy patterns of the lowest levels of the bulk entanglement spectrum. Our results demonstrate that anisotropic multispin interactions provide a versatile route to realizing higher-winding topological phases and unconventional multicriticality in one-dimensional quantum systems.

</details>


### [117] [Universal Driven Critical Dynamics of Entanglement Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03854)
*Chang-Yu Shen,Shuai Yin,Zi-Xiang Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子纠缠非平衡动力学的普适有限时间标度理论，通过无偏量子蒙特卡洛模拟研究(2+1)维相互作用狄拉克费米子从有序相驱动至量子临界点的角纠缠熵，发现其服从由驱动速率和系统尺寸控制的普适驱动标度，并可从非平衡动态中稳健提取共形场论的普适角系数，将Kibble-Zurek机制从局部观测量推广至非局域量子信息度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kibble-Zurek机制和有限时间标度是驱动临界动力学的基础框架，但其预测能力长期局限于局部观测量。量子纠缠作为一种本质上的非局域量子信息度量，能否在驱动临界动力学中遵循类似的普适标度规律尚不清楚，这构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 作者采用无偏量子蒙特卡洛模拟，针对(2+1)维相互作用狄拉克费米子，从有序相向量子临界点驱动，系统测量角纠缠熵随驱动速率和系统尺寸的演化行为，同时考察初始有序态分别为完全带隙态和含无隙Goldstone模两种情况。

Result: 研究发现角纠缠熵精确遵循由驱动速率和系统尺寸控制的普适驱动标度，且无论初始有序态是完全带隙还是含无隙Goldstone模均成立。关键的定量结果显示，动态纠缠对驱动速率呈对数依赖，由此可远超平衡态条件下稳健提取其下共形场论的普适角系数。

Conclusion: 该工作将Kibble-Zurek机制从局部观测量推广到本质非局域的量子信息度量，为在可编程量子模拟器上表征量子临界性和纠缠提供了实用蓝图。

Abstract: The Kibble-Zurek mechanism (KZM) and finite-time scaling (FTS) provide a foundational framework for driven critical dynamics, yet their predictive power has been largely confined to local observables. Here, we establish a universal finite-time scaling theory for the nonequilibrium dynamics of quantum entanglement. Using unbiased quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we investigate the corner entanglement entropy of (2+1)-dimensional interacting Dirac fermions driven from ordered phases toward a quantum critical point. We find that the corner entanglement accurately obeys a universal driven scaling governed by the driving rate and system size, persisting whether the initial ordered state is fully gapped or hosts gapless Goldstone modes. Crucially, this dynamical entanglement exhibits a logarithmic dependence on the driving rate, from which the universal corner coefficient of the underlying conformal field theory can be robustly extracted far from equilibrium. These results generalize the KZM from local observables to the intrinsic nonlocal quantum information measures, offering a practical blueprint for characterizing quantum criticality and entanglement on programmable quantum simulators.

</details>


### [118] [Evolution of electron spin resonance through a metallic quantum critical phase diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03994)
*Marc Scheffler,Jörg Sichelschmidt,Conrad Clauss,Mojtaba Javaheri Rahim,Boris I. Kochelaev,Cornelius Krellner,Christoph Geibel,Frank Steglich,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过一种新的实验方法，在超低温、宽频率和宽磁场范围内对重费米子金属YbRh2Si2进行电子自旋共振（ESR）测量，覆盖了从顺磁费米液体相到反铁磁有序相再到量子临界区的广大部分相图，发现ESR的g因子和线宽在三种相区表现出不同行为，为理解量子临界点物理提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重费米子金属YbRh2Si2中，抑制的反铁磁序附近的量子临界性由局域磁矩与巡游传导电子的相互作用所主导。如何在低温和宽参数范围内通过实验手段直接探测这一量子临界行为，是当前凝聚态物理中的重要问题。

Method: 开发了一种新的实验方法，使得在极低温和宽频率、宽磁场范围内能够进行电子自旋共振（ESR）测量，从而覆盖从顺磁费米液体相到反铁磁有序相以及量子临界区域的整个相图。

Result: 实验发现ESR的g因子和线宽在顺磁费米液体相、反铁磁有序相和量子临界区三个区域内呈现明显不同的行为。在磁场方向上直接冷却至量子临界点时，g因子和线宽均连续减小；此外，通过磁场调控和温度调控两种方式趋向量子临界点时，g因子的行为具有很好的匹配性。

Conclusion: 实验结果与现有强关联电子系统ESR理论进行了对比分析和讨论，为理解量子临界点附近的重费米子物理提供了重要的实验依据和新的见解。

Abstract: In the heavy-fermion metal YbRh2Si2, quantum criticality at a suppressed antiferromagnetic order is governed by the interplay of local magnetic moments and itinerant conduction electrons. We demonstrate how this can be investigated by a new experimental approach that enables the observation of electron spin resonance (ESR) across a broad range of frequencies and fields at very low temperatures. This allowed us to cover a large part of the phase diagram from the paramagnetic Fermi-liquid phase to the phase with antiferromagnetic order and including the quantum-critical regime. Both the ESR g-factor and the linewidth present distinct behaviors in these three regimes, providing further insight into the physics across a quantum critical point. Notably, when cooling down at a field directly towards the quantum critical point, both g-factor and linewidth continuously decrease. Furthermore, we observe a very good matching of the g-factor behavior upon field-tuning and temperature-tuning towards the quantum-critical point. We analyze and discuss the results in the context of present theories on ESR in strongly correlated electron systems.

</details>


### [119] [HyperDet Wavefunction: A Phase-Agnostic Ansatz for Strongly Correlated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04146)
*Xiaodong Hu,Guan-Lin Lin,Ying Ran,Di Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出超行列式（HyperDet）波函数，一种相位无关的变分拟设，通过可学习的融合张量将辅助费米子部分子Slater行列式融合为物理轨道，适用于玻色与费米强关联系统，可在整个分数量子霍尔（FCI）相及其邻近竞争相中达到≥99.9%的基态重叠，并能从融合张量中提取部分子拓扑数、投影对称性等场论信息，为强关联量子多体系统的基态搜索与相图探索提供统一平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述强关联系统的竞争相通常需要基于特定相假设构造试探波函数，缺乏相位无关的统一拟设；这限制了在不同量子相（自发对称破缺、分数化、拓扑有序）间进行无偏地搜索与探索相图的能力。

Method: 将辅助费米子部分子Slater行列式通过一个完全可学习的融合张量F融合到物理轨道中，构造相位无关的超行列式波函数；使用变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）优化；引入二分融合矩阵的奇异值谱作为诊断工具来追踪相变，并从优化后的融合张量中提取部分子拓扑数和投影对称性等拓扑数据。

Result: 单一HyperDet架构在玻色和费米模型中，对整个FCI相及其附近竞争相均达到≥99.9%的精确对角化基态重叠；融合张量奇异值谱的重分布可无偏地追踪多体相变；融合张量精确复现了玻色与费米FCI态的部分子Chern数，并恢复了玻色FCI的部分子平移分数化（投影类）。

Conclusion: HyperDet波函数是一个兼具高精度与可解释性的统一变分平台，既能进行精确的基态搜索和全相图探索，又能从部分子微观层面提供通往场论描述的深刻理论洞见。

Abstract: Describing competing phases of strongly correlated systems often requires trial wave functions built from phase-specific assumptions. We propose the \emph{hyperdeterminant (HyperDet) wavefunction} as a phase-agnostic ansatz for both bosonic and fermionic quantum many-body systems exhibiting spontaneous symmetry-breaking order, fractionalization, and/or topological order with anyonic excitations. The HyperDet structure emerges naturally by fusing auxiliary fermionic parton Slater determinants into physical orbitals through a fully learnable \emph{fusion tensor} $\mathcal F$. Optimized using variational Monte Carlo, a single HyperDet architecture can achieve exceptionally high overlaps $\geq 99.9\%$ with exact-diagonalization ground states throughout the entire fractional Chern insulator phase in both bosonic and fermionic models, and across their nearby competing phases. We introduce the singular-value spectrum of the \emph{bipartite fusion matrix} as a structural diagnostic of fusion tensor, and find that its redistribution tracks many-body phase transitions without computing phase-specific observables. The optimized fusion tensor also encodes the parton-level topological data: it reproduces the parton Chern numbers expected for the bosonic and fermionic FCI states, completing their field-theory descriptions and the resulting topological order. Its intrinsic gauge structure further determines whether physical symmetries admit virtual lifts and, when faithful lifts exist, extracts their projective class; for the bosonic FCI, this recovers the expected parton translation fractionalization. We thus anticipate the HyperDet wavefunction to be a promising variational platform for both accurate ground-state searches and phase-diagram explorations across strongly correlated phases, and for providing interpretable theoretical insights from parton-level microscopics to field-theory descriptions.

</details>


### [120] [Non-uniform quantum geometry stabilizes generalized Wigner crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04149)
*Nicolás Morales-Durán,Tobias M. R. Wolf,Jingtian Shi,Tomohiro Soejima,Andrew J. Millis,Jennifer Cano*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了Moiré材料中分数量子霍尔态（fractional Chern insulators）与电子晶体（generalized Wigner crystals）之间的竞争机制。作者在具有理想但动量相关量子几何的Chern能带（Aharonov-Casher能带）中，提出了广义Wigner晶体的试探波函数，并通过与竞争的Laughlin类态的能量比较，在填充因子ν=1/m下绘制了随几何非均匀性程度变化的相图。研究发现，量子几何控制的电荷密度零点涨落是决定晶体相对稳定性的关键机制，这暗示了一种量子Lindemann判据用于描述晶体-液相相边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Moiré材料中，分数量子霍尔态（FCI）和电子晶体（Wigner晶体）在能量上非常接近，但决定二者之间相选择的具体机制尚未明确。理解这一竞争关系对于预言和调控Moiré材料中的量子物态至关重要。

Method: 作者在具有理想但动量依赖的量子几何结构（即Aharonov-Casher能带）的Chern能带中开展研究。他们构建了一个针对广义Wigner晶体的解析试探波函数，并将其能量与竞争的Laughlin类似（分数量子霍尔）态的能量进行系统比较，从而在填充因子ν=1/m下建立相图，以几何非均匀度作为控制参数。

Result: 相图显示晶体与液相（分数量子霍尔态）的相对稳定性由量子几何控制的电荷密度零点涨落决定。广义Wigner晶体的这些零点涨落是调控其相对稳定性的微观机制，并由此推导出一个量子Lindemann判据，用于刻画晶体-液相的相边界位置。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了量子几何（而非仅Berry曲率）在Moiré材料中晶体与分数量子霍尔态的竞争中所起的关键作用，提出了一个量子Lindemann判据，为理解Moiré系统中电子晶体与拓扑流体的相选择提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Moiré materials host fractional Chern insulators and electron crystals in close proximity, but the mechanism selecting between them remains an open question. We address this competition in Chern bands with ideal but momentum-dependent quantum geometry -- Aharonov-Casher bands. We present an ansatz wave function for generalized Wigner crystals and, by comparing its energy to that of the competing Laughlin-like state, map out the phase diagram at filling fraction $ν=1/m$ as a function of the degree of geometric non-uniformity. Our work identifies quantum geometry-controlled zero point fluctuations of the charge density of the generalized Wigner crystal as the mechanism controlling its relative stability, implying a kind of quantum Lindemann criterion for the crystal-liquid phase boundary.

</details>


### [121] [Wavefunctions for Anyon Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04187)
*Donghae Seo,Taegon Lee,Gil Young Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个层级波函数构造框架，用于描述由父拓扑序派生的任意子超导态，通过等离子体类比和拓扑场论系统地刻画了超导态的离域长程序、凝聚电荷、手征中心荷及残余拓扑序，建立了任意子凝聚、超导序与拓扑场论之间的统一波函数级联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意子超导源于移动任意子的凝聚而非传统Cooper失稳，但此前缺乏系统的波函数描述。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个统一的波函数级框架来理解和构造任意子超导态。

Method: 开发层级波函数构造方案，从父拓扑序出发构造超导态；利用等离子体类比和拓扑场论来识别超导态的离域长程序、凝聚电荷、手征中心荷和残余拓扑序；构造了从semion态、ν=2/3层级态、ν=1/3 Laughlin态、ν=1整数量子霍尔态和Pfaffian态衍生的Abel和非Abel实例；在任意子希尔伯特空间公式的稀薄长距离极限中演示层级波函数自然涌现。

Result: 对于semion情形，多semion超导波函数等价于填充两个有效朗道能级的费米化任意子态，恢复了Laughlin关于semion超导体的原始构造。层级波函数可自然地从任意子希尔伯特空间公式的稀薄长距离极限中涌现，适用于理想Chern带和MoTe2扭转双层Moiré带。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个将任意子凝聚、超导序和拓扑场论统一起来的波函数级框架，为理解任意子超导态的微观结构提供了系统性的理论基础。

Abstract: Anyon superconductivity arises from the condensation of mobile anyons rather than from a conventional Cooper instability, yet a systematic wavefunction description remains lacking. We develop a hierarchy-wavefunction construction for superconducting states derived from parent topological orders and identify their off-diagonal long-range order, condensate charge, chiral central charge, and residual topological order through the plasma analogy and topological field theories. We construct Abelian and non-Abelian examples descending from the semion state, a $ν=2/3$ hierarchy state, the $ν=1/3$ Laughlin state, $ν=1$ integer quantum Hall state, and Pfaffian state. Remarkably, for the semion case, the superconducting many-semion wavefunction is equivalent to a state of fermionized anyons filling two effective Landau levels, recovering Laughlin's original construction of semion superconductor. Finally, we show that hierarchy wavefunctions emerge naturally in the dilute, long-distance limit of the anyon-Hilbert-space formulation, which applies to ideal Chern bands and moiré bands of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$. Our results establish a unified wavefunction-level framework linking anyon condensation, superconducting order, and topological field theory.

</details>

### [122] [Universal scaling of fluctuations and correlations across the superfluid transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03433)
*Paul Paquiez,Géraud Dupuy,Maxime Allemand,Henri Coquinot,Tommaso Roscilde,Nicolas Dupuis,Adam Rançon,Thomas Chalopin,David Clément*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究在超冷晶格玻色气体中实验验证了连续相变附近的普适标度行为：仅需两个系统特定的尺度因子，即可将不同熵下测量的序参量累积量塌缩到普适形式，并由此确定三个临界指数β、γ和ν，实验证明了多个可观测量同时遵循量子多体系统中的普适标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普适性是相变理论的核心预测，但此前实验仅验证了临界指数的幂律普适性，尚未完整验证"所有可观测量的普适标度函数仅由两个系统特定尺度因子完全决定"这一更深层预测，尤其在量子多体系统（如超冷原子）中缺乏实验演示。

Method: 在发生超流相变的超冷晶格玻色气体中，通过改变熵来测量序参量累积量；利用在不同熵下测量的累积量数据将其塌缩到普适形式，从而提取两个系统特定的尺度因子；然后利用这些尺度因子标定序参量分布和两点关联函数的普适标度函数。

Result: 成功提取了尺度因子，并证明这些因子能够统一描述序参量分布与两点关联函数的标度行为；通过测量这些标度函数，确定了三个临界指数β、γ和ν的实验值。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在量子多体系统中实验证明了多个可观测量的普适标度关系可同时成立，验证了普适标度函数超出简单幂律的更基本预测，深化了对量子相变普适性的理解。

Abstract: The critical behavior of a system near a continuous phase transition is commonly characterized by power laws and a set of universal critical exponents. Yet universality at phase transitions has even more fundamental manifestations. It dictates that all observables follow universal scaling functions, which may extend beyond simple power laws. Furthermore, microscopic details are captured entirely by only two system-specific scale factors, that rescale any measured quantity onto its universal form. In this work, we report the experimental validation of this prediction in ultracold lattice Bose gases undergoing the superfluid transition. We extract the scale factors by collapsing the order-parameter cumulants measured at varying entropies. We show that these factors specify scaling functions governing both the order-parameter distribution and two-point correlations. By measuring these scaling functions, we also determine three critical exponents $β$, $γ$ and $ν$. Our results provide an experimental demonstration that universal scaling is simultaneously obeyed by multiple observables in a quantum many-body system.

</details>


### [123] [Finite-temperature mass gap and quench dynamics of mobile impurities in a Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03656)
*Baihua Gong*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文将质量隙（mass-gap）模型推广到有限温度，结合Lee-Low-Pines变换与自洽Hartree-Fock退耦方法描述费米海中移动杂质的准粒子物理。有限温度下有效质量隙满足自洽方程Δ(T)=2U_F·tanh[Δ(T)/4k_BT]，在特征温度T*=U_F/(2k_B)以下存在非零解且以(T*-T)^{1/2}方式闭合，这标志着极化子和分子准粒子的热熔化。通过泛函行列式方法计算淬灭动力学，发现Ramsey响应的长时振荡（束缚态与隙内态之间的量子拍）恰好消失于T*之上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近提出的质量隙描述将Anderson正交性灾变与费米极化子准粒子图像通过反冲诱导能隙联系起来，但该描述局限于零温且不涉及动力学。作者旨在将这一模型推广至有限温度并研究其淬灭动力学，以建立热力学与动力学可观测现象之间的统一联系。

Method: 采用Lee-Low-Pines变换结合自洽Hartree-Fock退耦来处理反冲诱导相互作用；运用泛函行列式方法研究相互作用突然淬灭后的量子动力学。关键理论结果是通过自洽方程Δ(T)=2U_F tanh[Δ(T)/4k_BT]确定温度依赖的有效质量隙。

Result: 自洽方程在T* = U_F/(2k_B)以下给出非零质量隙解，其闭合行为遵循(T*-T)^{1/2}标度律，被识别为极化子与分子准粒子热熔化的平均场标志。Ramsey响应S(t)在低温下的长时量子拍振荡精确地在T*以上消失，将热力学和动力学指纹统一于单一的温度依赖平均场参数。

Conclusion: 该工作将极化子形成的热力学与动力学特征（准粒子热熔化与量子拍消失）联系到同一个温度依赖的平均场参数上，为理解有限温度下费米极化子系统的准粒子稳定性提供了统一的定量框架。

Abstract: Recently, a mass-gap description of mobile impurities in a Fermi gas was introduced, which connects Anderson's orthogonality catastrophe for static impurities to the quasiparticle picture of Fermi polarons through a recoil-induced energy gap in the fermionic dispersion. That description, however, was restricted to zero temperature and did not address dynamics. Here we generalize the mass-gap model to finite temperature by combining the Lee--Low--Pines transformation with a self-consistent Hartree--Fock decoupling of the recoil-induced interaction, and we study the quench dynamics within this framework using the functional-determinant approach. At finite temperature the effective mass gap obeys the self-consistency equation $Δ(T)=2U_F\tanh[Δ(T)/4k_B T]$, with $U_F=k_F^2/2M$ and $M$ the impurity mass. This equation admits a nonzero solution below the characteristic temperature $T^*=U_F/(2k_B)$ and closes as $(T^*-T)^{1/2}$. We identify this closing as the mean-field signature of the thermal melting of the polaron and molecule quasiparticles. Computing the Ramsey response $S(t)$ after a sudden quench of the impurity--fermion interaction, we find that its long-time oscillations---quantum beats between the bound and in-gap states---disappear precisely above $T^*$. Our work ties the thermodynamic and dynamical fingerprints of polaron formation to a single temperature-dependent mean-field parameter.

</details>


### [124] [Three-observable measurement of the critical velocity in a paraxial superfluid of light flowing past a mobile impurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03948)
*T. Aladjidi,M. Baker-Rasooli,T. D. Ferreira,A. Bramati,M. Albert,Q. Glorieux,P. -É. Larré*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文报道了在87Rb热原子蒸汽中利用双波长光学方案实现的平行超流体中，测量可移动杂质临界速度的实验。通过同时监测涡旋数量、流体动力力和位移速度三种互补可观测量，三种独立方法得到一致的临界速度值，且系统性低于Landau判据并随杂质半径减小而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子流体动力学中的杂质问题是理解超流耗散、量子湍流和Landau判据适用性的核心问题。此前缺乏一个可控性强、可调节参数丰富的实验平台来系统研究有限质量、大半径移动杂质在二维超流中的临界行为，因此需要一种多功能平台来探索这一物理。

Method: 采用双波长光学方案在87Rb热蒸汽中实现二维平行光子超流体，构造强排斥势、大可调半径且有限质量的移动杂质。通过三个互补可观测量（尾流中涡旋产生数、流体动力力、杂质相对流速的位移速度）同时评估临界速度，并进行交叉验证。

Result: 三种独立诊断方法得到的临界速度值一致；临界速度系统性低于Landau判据预测值，并且随着杂质半径的增大而减小，与理论预期定性吻合。

Conclusion: 联合诊断方案的可靠性得到验证，确立了平行光子超流体作为探索量子流体力学中杂质问题的多功能实验平台。

Abstract: We report the measurement of the critical velocity of a paraxial superfluid of light flowing past a mobile impurity, realized using a dual-wavelength optical scheme in a warm vapor of $^{87}\text{Rb}$. The superflow is two-dimensional by nature, while the impurity features a highly repulsive potential, a large tunable radius, and a finite mass. The critical velocity is evaluated by simultaneously monitoring three complementary observables: the number of vortices nucleated in the wake of the impurity, the hydrodynamic force exerted on it, and its displacement velocity relative to the flow. These independent methods yield consistent values for the critical velocity, which remain systematically below the Landau criterion and decrease with the impurity radius, in qualitative agreement with theoretical expectations. Our results demonstrate the reliability of these combined diagnostics, establishing paraxial superfluids of light as a versatile platform for exploring the impurity problem in quantum hydrodynamics.

</details>
